<div class="index-big wow fadeInLeft" data-wow-delay="300ms" data-wow-duration="900ms">
<h1>Willkommen</h1>
<div class="txt textstyles text-word-wrap">
                                    ...bei Campingplus.de - Der Online-Campingshop<br/>Wir sind Fachhändler für Camping-, Caravan- und Freizeitzubehör.<br/>In unserem Onlineshop <a href="http://www.campingplus.de">Campingplus.de</a> sind Sie an der richtigen Adresse um alles für Ihren Campingurlaub zu entdecken.<br/>Finden Sie z.B. <a href="Zelte-Markisen/Caravan-Zelte.html"><b>Caravan-Vorzelte</b></a> der führenden Zelthersteller und <a href="Elektro-TV-Solar/Antennen-u.-TV/Antennen.html">autom. Satellitenanlagen</a> in unserem umfangreichen Sortiment passend für Ihre Ansprüche.<br/>Angefangen vom Zelthering über Trekkingzelte und Campinggeschirr bis zu 
TV  und Solaranlagen finden Sie bei uns laufend interessante Angebote. Wir sind übrigens auch Hobby-Wohnwagen Vertragshändler und können Ihnen auch <a href="http://www.wohnwageninfo.de" target="_blank">HIER</a> weiterhelfen.<br/>

<!--  Aktivierung UVP, Infozeile, Artikelnr -->
<script data-cfasync="false" type="text/javascript">
        var _thousandsRE = new RegExp('(-?[0-9]+)([0-9]{3})');

        function escapeHTML(s) {
            var repl = function(c) { return HTML_MAP[c]; };
            return s.replace(/[&<>'"]/g, repl); // Replace [&<>"'] using the function
        };


        /**
         * Creates the HTML for a single grid/list result.
         * @param doc the document object
         * @param absolutePos the absolute position (takes into account paging)
         * @param price the formatted price
         * @param docUrl the document URL with [&'] replaced by HTML entities
         * @param titleAttr the document title with [&'] replaced by HTML entities
         * @param showList if true, the list view is being displayed
         * @returns the inner HTML for the given document
         */
        function custom_makeRow(doc, absolutePos, price, docUrl, titleAttr, showList) {

            // example howto extract an extra display fields "uvp", "mpn" and "infozeile"

            var uvp="", mpn="", infozeile="";
            var words = doc.Display.split("\u00a6");
            for (var i = 0; i < words.length; i++) {
                key_val = words[i].split('=');
                switch (key_val[0]) {
                    case "uvp": uvp = key_val[1]; break;
                    case "mpn": mpn = key_val[1]; break;
                    case "infozeile": infozeile = key_val[1]; break;
                    default: break;
                }
            }

            var imgAnchor = "";
            if (doc.Image) {
                imgAnchor = "<a class='exo-prod-url' rel='" + absolutePos
                    + "' href='" + docUrl + "' aid='" + escapeHTML(doc.AID)
                    + "' title='" + titleAttr + "'></a>";
            }
            var innerHtml = "<div class='exo-img'><div>" + imgAnchor + "</div></div>";

            innerHtml += "<div class='exo-desc'><div class='exo-prodName'>"
                + "<a class='exo-prod-url' rel='" + absolutePos + "' href='"
                + docUrl + "' aid='" + escapeHTML(doc.AID) + "' title='" + titleAttr + "'>"
                + titleAttr + "</a></div>";

            // Display the infozeile only if one is defined for the record
            //     if (infozeile) {
            //          innerHtml += "<div class='exo-infozeile'>" + infozeile + "</div>";
            //     }
            // Display the descript only if this is a list row
            if (showList) {
                innerHtml += "<div class='exo-prodDesc'>"
                 <!--   + doc.Description + -->
				 "</div>";
            }

            if (price) {
                innerHtml += "<div class='exo-prodPrice'>" + price + "</div>";
            }

            // Display the uvp only if one is defined for the record
            //    if (uvp.length > 0) {
            //        while (_thousandsRE.test(uvp)) {
            //            uvp=uvp.replace(_thousandsRE, '$1' + ',' + '$2');
            //        }
            //       innerHtml += "<div class='exo-oldprice'>"
            //            +uvp.replace('.',',') + " &euro;</div>";
            //    }
            // Display the mpn only if one is defined for the record
            if (mpn.length > 0) {
                innerHtml += "<div class='exo-mpn'>Art.Nr.&nbsp;" + mpn + infozeile + "</div>";
            }

            innerHtml += "</div>";

            return innerHtml;
        };

        function custom_ecs_formatListRow(doc, absolutePos, price, docUrl, titleAttr) {
            return custom_makeRow(doc, absolutePos, price, docUrl, titleAttr, true);
        }

        function custom_ecs_formatGridRow(doc, absolutePos, price, docUrl, titleAttr) {
            return custom_makeRow(doc, absolutePos, price, docUrl, titleAttr, false);
        }

        var userExoSearchParams = {
            formatGridRow : custom_ecs_formatGridRow,
            formatListRow : custom_ecs_formatListRow
        };

        function custom_ecs_postProcess () {
            $exoJ("#mobile-search").removeClass('open animated fadeIn');
        }
    </script>
